### Karoubi envelope

- Canonical form: object P; morphisms are clamped matrices with QAP=A

### Face formulas and BDDs

- Canonical form: ROBDD trees expanded from interned DAGs (compatibility view); primary decisions use manager roots
- Categorification: canonical representatives of the face lattice (a quotient of the free bounded distributive lattice)

### Dependent NbE (Π/Σ/universes)

- Canonical form: η-long dependent normal forms quoted by type; Π/Σ second components are semantic closure families; cumulative Typeᵢ levels
- Categorification: a categories-with-families (CwF) model: context extension and dependent substitution are closure application
- Decision: bidirectional elaboration plus type-directed conversion; equality is normal-form equality; a term-level strengthening case-splits on discrete Bool context variables, difference-guided by the two normal forms and reporting split nodes/probes (recursive decision after both substitutions, dependent types substituted along; Nat variables are never split) without changing kernel conversion

### Cubical AST and dimension NbE

- Canonical form: separate term/dimension de Bruijn indices in DTerm; line and tube bodies; quoting with dlevel
- Categorification: a cubical CwF with interval contexts; PathP is the dependent path object and Glue values are (raw/DPartial partial, base) pairs

### AST Kan comp/hcomp

- Canonical form: branches are always carried as DSystem; dispatch by type head at a generic dimension; top branches give caps and formal DNComp/DNHComp are the stuck forms; CircleSquareWindingReport records 2D circle boundary windings and H-space gluing status
- Categorification: every type former preserves Kan fibration structure; hcompU is Glue gluing; the groupoid H-space multiplication on S¹≃K(ℤ,1) and T²≃S¹×S¹ reduces 0~1 endpoint-glued 2D open-box boundaries to an integer 2-cocycle winding conservation condition
- Decision: system base/overlap compatibility plus recursion on types; 2D endpoint-glued circle/torus open boxes construct H-space rotation caps at zero net winding and return None on non-zero winding obstructions; coe/fill are segment restrictions of comp

### Persistent environments, laziness and semantic caches

- Canonical form: length-carrying persistent lists DEnv/DDimEnv (innermost de Bruijn head); DVThunk is forced at most once; cache keys are simplified dimensions / quoted-term Debug keys; global DTerm hash-consing builds an intern table bottom-up from subterm stamps so structurally equal subterms share one intern id while rebuilt terms stay structurally equal; the intern table also stores child ids per node, DTermDagSnapshot exports that same DAG as local shells plus child-id edges, and CHDAGBIN v1 imports it by per-node hash-consing with added/reused counts
- Categorification: CwF context extension is cons with a shared tail; lazy values are call-by-need semantic values and memoized families/dimension lines are finite graphs of extensional functions
- Decision: environment indexing, force_count and cache-key equality

### Dependent Kan lines (coe/comp)

- Canonical form: DLine{at, coe, fill} with comp=fill(1); coe goes backwards in Π domains and along the first-component path in Σ
- Categorification: a type is a Kan fibration structure; a dependent family is (path)→DLine; together with subspace/Glue lines all project type families are covered
- Decision: recursion on type formers; coe(r,r) is definitionally the identity

### BDD DAGs, budgets and DPLL

- Canonical form: hash-consed DAG node ids; the reachable-node count of a root is its ROBDD size; LRAT proof steps are indexed LratAdd/LratDelete chains with a CHLRATBN v1 binary representation
- Categorification: shared canonical graphs of boolean functions at a fixed variable order; apply is a memoized lattice operation, and DRAT→LRAT elaboration lifts satisfiability-preserving traces to locally checkable proof chains
- Decision: same-manager root-id equality, sat as root≠0, node-budget FaceDecision, or step-budgeted DPLL (the legacy scanner and the watched-literal engine on Tseitin CNF); candidate-order minimization, bounded sifting, apply-cache doubling rehash, and a 3ⁿ worst-case node bound; CDCL adds 1-UIP clause learning, VSIDS, Luby restarts, LBD clause deletion, DRUP/DRAT checking, backward core trimming, DRAT→LRAT elaboration and linear-time indexed LRAT checking; provably optimal variable orders combine heuristic upper-bound branch-and-bound, cofactor symmetry-orbit pruning (single component up to n≤20) and variable-cooccurrence connected-support decomposition (disjoint support components solved exactly and concatenated; single components above cap report exact=false)

### Raw DSystem compatibility and first-match normal forms

- Canonical form: compatible systems reuse the type-directed DSystem normal form; arbitrary first-match systems become disjoint effective regions DTubeNF; agreement regions of two values come in both boolean BDD form and a Face-valued form retaining both endpoints, and exact repair truncates branches to the Face-valued agreement without touching endpoints; DSystemDsaturCliqueReport records chromatic upper bounds and max-clique pruning statistics
- Categorification: partial elements in face-relative slices; the checked constructor is a reflection into the composition-system subclass; the chromatic number χ(G[P]) is a dual upper bound on the clique number ω(G[P])
- Decision: local-context conversion enumerates only the dimensions actually mentioned by the branches (irrelevant dimensions are pruned by BDD cofactor structure), checking the base and pairwise overlaps; exact repair and maximum completion enumerate maximum compatible subsystems by exact maximum clique or DSATUR saturation-degree chromatic upper-bound pruning

### First-class AST Equiv and GlueEquiv

- Canonical form: DEquiv carries fwd/bwd/sect/retr terms; DVGlueEquiv carries full equivalence branches and (partial, base) pairs
- Categorification: equivalences in the AST are isomorphisms with homotopy inverse proofs; ua is a functor to universe paths and GlueEquiv glues along systems of partial equivalences
- Decision: bidirectional checking of the four components and two PathP proofs; conversion/quotation recurse over equivalence components and branch faces; coe along ua is definitionally fwd/bwd

### Checked semantic type lines

- Canonical form: DCheckedTypeLine stores a checked open type body, contexts, environments and a universe level, never a raw host function
- Categorification: substitutable cubical type lines are syntactic bodies evaluated in dimension environments and therefore commute with dimension substitution
- Decision: the body is checked at a fresh dimension against Typeᵢ; at evaluates by dimension and coe/comp dispatch to AST Kan operations

### Schema registry and elaboration caches

- Canonical form: DInductivePlan/DHitPlan interned in DSchemaRegistry by stable Debug keys, with telescope prefix chains; the CHREGBIN v2 byte form is magic + version + length + checksum + the DAG DTerm/schema codec payload shared with CHMODBIN v3 (v1 remains readable)
- Categorification: schema descriptors are shareable plans; caches store only finite graphs of the same elaboration functions and do not change denotation
- Decision: validation booleans and semantic telescope prefixes are looked up by (term,args,args) keys; hit/miss counters are observable

### Schema functors, positivity and induction-recursion

- Canonical form: the Id/Const/Product/Sum/List/Pi/Container/DecodedFunction/Family/SelfFamily grammar of DPositiveFunctor and its decoded types; DPositivityReport records occurrences and polarities; DFunctorialPositivityCert carries semantic covariant-law or container-isomorphism certificates
- Categorification: initial algebras of positive type functors with decoded induction hypotheses; DDecode is the decoding function of a Tarski universe; polynomial container isomorphisms Equiv(F X, Σs:S.P(s)→X) give an anti-circular semantic characterization of strictly positive endofunctors
- Decision: polarity recursion on alias-expanded DTerms: nested functors and positive positions are accepted, negative positions rejected, and unknown neutral family applications rejected unless backed by a DPositivityDeclaration, a lambda-verified operator, or a kernel-checked semantic certificate (anti-circular container isomorphisms grant strict_args, whereas covariant functor laws alone grant only non-strict positive_args); IHs are generated by functor recursion

### Schema extensions: mutual induction, quotients and universe polymorphism

- Canonical form: the Sum-indexed merged schema of DMutualFamily; level schemes DLevelExpr; quotients are HITs with equality 1-cells
- Categorification: mutual families are one initial algebra over an index sum; a universe scheme is a function of level assignments; quotients are higher cellular gluing
- Decision: mutual constructors compile with component index constraints and eliminate with Σ motives; schemes evaluate on level vectors and can be revalidated; constructor equations are checked as Path constraints

### Exact univariate polynomials and rational matrices

- Canonical form: dense low-to-high QPoly coefficients with trailing zeros removed; QMat rational row lists; RREF and pivot columns
- Categorification: the Euclidean ring Q[x] and the linear category over Qⁿ; characteristic/minimal polynomials are representation invariants of matrix algebras
- Decision: multiplication is sparse-convolution or common-denominator BigInt integer scaling with schoolbook/Karatsuba kernels and binary exponentiation; primitive pseudo-remainder gcd, Yun squarefree factorization, the rational-root theorem, Faddeev–LeVerrier and RREF; all equality is rational structural equality

### Algebraic extensions and radical normal forms

- Canonical form: QuadNum a+b√d with squarefree d; CubicNum a+b∛m+c∛m² with cubefree m; TriquadNum on the 8-element basis of three squareclass-independent generators; MultiQuadNum on the 16-element basis of up to four generators, aligned by squareclass span; AlgNum remainder polynomials in Q[x]/(m)
- Categorification: quadratic and general finite algebraic extension fields; norm/trace come from the determinant/trace of multiplication matrices
- Decision: residue arithmetic modulo a minimal polynomial and extended-Euclid inversion; multiquadratic inversion by Galois-conjugate products and sign by dyadic interval refinement; radical denesting by square tests; equality is coefficient equality after basis alignment

### Generalized eigenspaces and Jordan theory

- Canonical form: nullity chains of powers of (A−λI), Jordan block sizes and chain bases; PJP⁻¹=A
- Categorification: primary decomposition is a resolution of the identity from coprime polynomial factors; Jordan type is a complete invariant fragment of similarity
- Decision: exact decision from characteristic-polynomial multiplicities, kernel dimensions of powers and chain lifting; non-split cases over Q report residual factors and AlgMat continues over extensions

### Certified numeric shadows and exact cubes

- Canonical form: QuadC=QuadNum+i·QuadNum; CertMat and degree-≤2 CertPath/CertSquare coefficients
- Categorification: exact complex quadratic matrix algebra and polynomial cubes; Double matrices are only approximate representations of exact shadows
- Decision: P²=P, P†=P, orthogonal decompositions and box boundaries are checked by exact structural equality; numeric agreement measures only deviation from shadows and floating residuals

### Spheres and suspension HITs

- Canonical form: north/south points of parameterized SuspS(A) with one meridian per point a:A; S²=Susp(S¹), S³=Susp(S²); Susp(Bool)≃S¹, Cocone(A,B) and the pointed loop-suspension adjunction (ΣA→*B)≃(A→*ΩB)
- Categorification: suspension is the homotopy-pushout HIT quotient collapsing both ends of A×I to points, classified by cocone universal property; Σ and Ω form the canonical homotopy adjunction Σ⊣Ω on pointed types
- Decision: generic HIT schema validation, meridian endpoint face reduction, dependent elimination and hcomp; 0-connected spaces have trivial suspended fundamental group ‖Ω¹(Susp(A))‖₀≃1 by the 0-connected Freudenthal suspension theorem (instantiating π₁(S²)≃1 and π₁(S³)≃1), complemented by circle wedge-connectivity extension and coherences

### Jacobi interval spectral iteration

- Canonical form: RatInterval/CInterval rectangular enclosures and dyadically outward-rounded Hermitian interval matrices; every rotation stores before/after matrices and the accumulated unitary transform
- Categorification: monotone lifting of Hermitian similarity transforms to interval-matrix enclosures; Gershgorin discs give spectral image decompositions and multiplicity clusters
- Decision: exact rational square comparisons certify sqrt/phase/c/s enclosures; every rotation recomputes J†J−I∋0 and J†AJ⊆after, and final discs are clustered and counted; eigenvalue clusters are separated by Sturm-counting the squarefree part (Sturm chain with derivative gcd) and matching multiplicities back by unique interval overlap; the interval iteration itself is cluster-aware: Gershgorin clusters are detected during iteration, a cluster penalty biases pivot choice, precision escalates and reruns when insufficient, and a count-2 cluster is separated into disjoint singletons inside the iteration; an adaptive driver switches to exact characteristic-polynomial separation when the Jacobi envelopes stagnate, retaining exact multiplicities

### Higher-order unification and the bidirectional elaborator

- Canonical form: Miller-pattern spines `?F x₁…xₙ` in UTerm and beta/projection-reduced zonked cores; surface STerm resolves names to de Bruijn UTerm; HouDelayedGraphReport stores delayed Huet flex-flex constraint-graph edges, and HouLazyUnifierStream stores an explicit pull-based continuation stack
- Categorification: pattern HOU is the decidable fragment of higher-order unification; Huet pre-unification quotients by delayed flex-flex equations and lazy streams unfold the search tree coalgebraically; bidirectional elaboration sends implicit arguments and dependent motives to the same unifier and compiles to the dependent core
- Decision: rigid decomposition after WHNF, pattern abstraction, occurs/scope checks, bounded Huet search, three-state Miller-pattern MGU, cross-constraint residual solving, Huet delayed flex-flex graph wake-up and pre-unifier reification, and pull-based lazy normal-form unifier streams

### Parameterized modules and namespaces

- Canonical form: module telescopes (parameters→imports and local declarations interleaved as ModuleItem entries; the legacy decls-only construction still reads, with all imports front-loaded) with separate name→ordinal bindings; instances are simultaneously substituted closed declarations
- Categorification: parameterized modules are module functors, import is inlined functor application, and open/alias/rename are name projections of one telescope entry
- Decision: cycle detection, dependent checking of parameter arguments, ambiguity rejection for import selection/hiding/renaming, neutralization of opaque exports, and dep_check of every closed instance declaration

### Cubical algorithms

- Canonical form: KanBox packages a line, a system and a base; transport fillers are PathP values; path transitivity is the top edge of an hcomp square; Σ-path splitting, fibration loop equivalences and Stasheff's 5D associahedron K₅ coherence 5-cell
- Categorification: path-object operations generated by Kan fibration structure in a cubical CwF, fibration loop sequences Ω¹(ΣB P)≃Σ(p:Ω¹B).(subst P p e₀≡e₀) and the K₅ 5-cell classifying five-fold A_∞ loop associativity
- Decision: dispatch to kernel coe/comp/fill with endpoint conversion; unit-law 3-cells, Mac Lane pentagon 4-cells and the Stasheff K₅ 5-cell between northern and southern pentagon routes are verified by conversion; closed and open certified equivalences are checked by the dependent checker

### Computational univalence

- Canonical form: finite universe paths are normalized by wiring equivalences; dependent universe paths are checked type lines, pathToEquiv is their coe equivalence, ua is the Glue line, uaβ unglues a transport filler, and the round-trips are an Equiv homotopy and a 2D Glue square
- Categorification: an equivalence (Equiv A B) ≃ Path U A B between the path category of the universe and equivalences; Glue glues along equivalences and transport is its coe functor
- Decision: wiring equality in the finite layer; coe/comp computation and type-directed conversion of round-trip endpoints and square edges in the AST layer; closed-term pathToEquiv fill/coe proofs are checked by the dependent checker

### Complete exact algebra and root isolation

- Canonical form: BigInt rationals; irreducible factors and multiplicities in Q[x]; exact rational LLL-reduced lattice bases with unimodular transforms U∈GLₙ(ℤ); Van Hoeij power-sum trace-lattice factors; Sturm real-root intervals, disjoint Rouché complex disks and complex algebraic closure elements ComplexAlgNum∈Q̄⊂ℂ; biquadratic numbers and denested radical expressions
- Categorification: unique factorization in Q[x], GLₙ(ℤ) lattice-reduction orbits, Frobenius/Hensel power-sum trace sublattices and the complex-embedded direct limit of the algebraic closure Q̄⊂ℂ
- Decision: certificate fast-path irreducibility, Hensel lifting with Fujiwara-bounded Van Hoeij LLL trace-lattice recombination (avoiding exponential combinatorics on Swinnerton-Dyer polynomials) and Kronecker fallback; Sturm counting, Cauchy/Rouché disk refinement and resultant elimination implement exact field arithmetic, conjugation, nth roots and equality in Q̄⊂ℂ

### First-class universe-polymorphic and simultaneous declaration schemas

- Canonical form: level binders and instantiation in DLevelDecl; positive functors extracted after transparent-operator unfolding; iteratively compiled non-uniform nested families; joint mutual, cumulative cross-universe simultaneous IR and Dybjer–Setzer open multi-decoder simultaneous IR DOpenSimultaneousIRSpec
- Categorification: universe schemes, parameter-iterated initial-algebra families, cumulative Σ joint motives and open synchronous induction-recursion initial algebras over multi-slice fiber categories Famₘ(Type)
- Decision: level scope, positivity reports, generated telescopes and dependent conversion; simultaneous IR compiles mutually dependent cross-universe large/small blocks at joint_motive_level = maxᵢ function_universe_level(fᵢ); open multi-decoder simultaneous IR supports validation, code checking, decoding, dependent elimination and single-decoder kernel lowering

### Adjoint equivalences, fibers and propositional truncation

- Canonical form: a triangle Path(Path) over a quasi-inverse; the improved counit; Σ-points of fibers with singleton contraction; the point/squash HIT for PropTrunc; the point/hub/spoke set truncation ‖A‖₀ (SetTruncS) and open-variable bidirectional bijectivity certification DVEquiv
- Categorification: half-adjoint equivalences, the contractible-fiber characterization of equivalences, and left-adjoint truncation reflections into propositions and into h-sets (0-groupoids)
- Decision: adjointify_equiv_value, equiv_bidirectional_certified_in and equiv_contractible_fibers_value construct and check half-adjoint equivalences in open variable contexts; SetTruncS carries an internal h-set proof, universal elimination into arbitrary h-sets, and ‖X‖₀≃B equivalence lifting

### Spherical homotopy representatives and the Hopf invariant

- Canonical form: nested Path types for Ωⁿ; integer degree maps by iterated suspension; the pole-swapped S³ HIT; geometric and internal univalent Hopf fibration H:S²→Type₀ with total space E_Hopf=Σ(x:S²)H(x); canonical IntegerS integers with an internal h-set proof; π₂(S²)=‖Ω²(S²)‖₀ and π₃ quotient presentations
- Categorification: suspension functors, the fibration loop sequence Ω¹(E_Hopf)≃Σ(p:Ω¹(S²))Path(S¹,subst H p base,base) and the Hopf–Freudenthal identity retraction ∂_Hopf(σ_{S²}(p))≡p along suspended loops
- Decision: groupoid laws, Ω² Eckmann–Hilton interchange and pentagon 4-cells verified face by face; the Hopf total-space loop equivalence is bidirectionally certified, and ∂_Hopf(σ_{S²}(p))≡p is proved over an open neutral loop p:Ω¹(S¹), yielding a certified split-retract section from ℤ into π₂(S²) over an open integer variable

### Separate module compilation and linking

- Canonical form: flattened checked telescopes, exports, dependency fingerprints and public interface projections of CompiledModule; CHMODBIN v1–v3 / TLV envelopes and shared DAG term codecs; topological build plans and incremental relink reports; ArtifactRepository with FIPS 180-4 SHA-256/512, RFC 8032 Pure-Ed25519 CHARTSIG asymmetric sealing, CHWALJRN crash-consistent write-ahead logging and LCA-based three-way merge reports
- Categorification: compiled objects of module functors, signature-quotient-invariant incremental relinking, read-only public-key verification morphisms, write-ahead atomic commit monads and three-way pushout merges relative to lowest common ancestors
- Decision: interface hashes omit opaque bodies so downstream modules skip recompilation and relink via module_instantiate; SHA-256/512 and RFC 8032 Ed25519 enforce anti-forgery and non-malleability; CHWALJRN rolls back uncommitted transactions and torn tails via SHA-256 frame checksums with idempotent replay; three-way merges support convergent edits, tombstone propagation and three conflict policies

### The circle's universal cover and encode/decode

- Canonical form: cover fibres are canonical IntegerS integers over base and ua-line GlueEquiv types over loop points; loop representatives are DVHitCell paths of the schema circle, winding numbers are canonical integer values, and π₁(S¹)=‖Ω¹(S¹)‖₀≃ℤ is certified as a DVEquiv
- Categorification: the helix is a fibration family from the circle to the universe; encode is the endpoint of substitution (path lifting) in the cover, decode is integer elimination into loop powers, and set truncation lifts Ω¹(S¹)≃ℤ to the fundamental-group isomorphism via ℤ being an h-set
- Decision: the base fibre is convertible to ℤ at universe level; substitution accumulates winding numbers across composite loops; both homotopies decode(encode(p))≡p and encode(decode(z))≡z are constructed internally in open neutral loop and integer contexts, completing the full internal proofs of Path S¹ base x ≃ Cover(x), Ω¹(S¹)≃ℤ and π₁(S¹)=‖Ω¹(S¹)‖₀≃ℤ

### Host-opaque cubes and open-box filling

- Canonical form: cubes are host functions (dimension lists to semantic values / rational-polynomial coordinates) or CheckedDCube terms anchored to typechecked syntax; an open box is boundary data missing one face and the cap is the filler's restriction to that face
- Categorification: external cubical data over □ with a host implementation of the Kan filling condition, whose natural transformations are determined at the generic diagonal by the cubical Yoneda lemma; polynomial cubes are cubical maps in exact rational coordinates, filled by the iteration F += w·(S − F|face) face by face
- Decision: boundary completeness and overlap compatibility are verified face by face by typed conversion / canonical polynomial equality; extensional comparison combines four-tier stratified probes, strength t≤4 orthogonal/covering arrays over P¹(𝔽₈) with a 5-color perfect hash family, a cubical Yoneda naturality/faithfulness audit that rejects non-natural host closures, and exact probe-free generic-dimension reification for CheckedDCube

### Topos: sites, sieves, Grothendieck topologies and presheaves

- Canonical form: sorted deduplicated morphism sieves, covering-sieve closures, natural-transformation component tables and matching families

### Topos: sheafification, cartesian closedness, subobject classifiers and elementary toposes

### Topos: geometric morphisms and local/connected/atomic/compact-coherent properties

- Canonical form: left-exact inverse images f^* = a ∘ Lan_{u^{op}}, right-adjoint direct images f_*, flat tensor point functors and focal points
- Categorification: geometric morphism adjunctions f^* ⊣ f_*, local-topos triple adjunctions Δ ⊣ Γ ⊣ Γ^!, π_0 connectedness, atomic Galois groupoids and coherent compact coverings

### Topos: geometric normal forms, classifying toposes and categorification

### Topos: Round-2 structural derivations (essential triple adjunctions, open/closed subtopos nuclei, dense Comparison Lemma, internal torsors and cup-product ring)

- Canonical form: right Kan presheaves Ran_{u^{op}}, open/closed Lawvere–Tierney nuclei j_o/j_c, induced dense subsite topologies J_C, internal permutation-group actions and Alexander–Whitney 2-cochain cup products
- Categorification: essential geometric morphisms Lan_{u^{op}} ⊣ u^* ⊣ Ran_{u^{op}}, co-Heyting open/closed subtopos complementarity, Grothendieck–Verdier dense site Morita equivalence, Diaconescu internal G-torsor sheaves and multiplicative DG-algebras H^•(U; Q)
- Decision: triple-adjunction hom bijections, J_o ∧ J_c = J_triv & J_o ∨ J_c = J_⊥ complementarity without internal LEM, dense sheaf reconstruction, shear bijections T × G ≅ T × T and Koszul-signed Leibniz rules separating T^2 from S^1 ∨ S^1 ∨ S^2

- Categorification: principal divisor quotients, Grothendieck–Serre dualizing complexes ω_{P^1} ≅ O(-2)[1], ω_{P^2} ≅ O(-3)[2] and ω_C ≅ O_C(d-3)[1], Yoneda functors of points h_X : CRing → Set and multi-projective Gröbner Chow motives
